--- yogas ---
  [nbrj-Su] Neechabhanga Raja Yoga  planets=Su
      Sun's debilitation is cancelled because Saturn, exalted in that very sign's field, occupies a kendra; and the lord of its exaltation sign, Mars, occupies a kendra from Lagna. What begins as the chart's weakest point matures into a source of unusual strength, typically after early-life struggle in that portfolio.
  [nbrj-Mo] Neechabhanga Raja Yoga  planets=Mo
      Moon's debilitation is cancelled because its dispositor Mars occupies a kendra from Lagna; and its dispositor stands in a kendra from the Moon. What begins as the chart's weakest point matures into a source of unusual strength, typically after early-life struggle in that portfolio.
  [vrj-Ve] Vipareeta Raja Yoga (Harsha)  planets=Ve
      Venus, lord of the 6th, sits in the 6th — the ruler of a difficult house (the 6th, 8th and 12th are the "dusthana" houses) sitting in another difficult house, which the tradition reads as a reversal. Adversity turns on itself: rivals self-destruct, crises resolve in your favour, and gains arrive through difficulty others cannot stomach.
  [chandra-mangala] Chandra-Mangala Yoga  planets=Mo,Ma
      Moon and Mars conjoin — an earning combination: entrepreneurial drive fused with instinct. Wealth through self-effort, with a temper that needs channels, not suppression.
  [mahapurusha-Ma] Ruchaka Yoga (Pancha Mahapurusha)  planets=Ma
      Mars is in its own sign and occupies the 7th house, one of the four corner houses (a 'kendra') — one of the five Mahapurusha ("great person") combinations. It stamps the whole personality with a commanding, athletic physicality and fearless executive nerve. You lead from the front, thrive in competition, defence, surgery or engineering, and carry authority that is taken rather than granted. Anger is the tax on the gift.
  [yk-Sa] Yogakaraka in Strength  planets=Sa
      Saturn, the most helpful planet for your rising sign (the 'yogakaraka' — it rules a corner house and a fortunate house at once), occupies the 4th house. A single-planet engine of rank: its main periods (dashas) carry the chart's biggest promotions in status and material standing.
  [raja-Ma-Me] Raja Yoga (Kendra–Trikona link)  planets=Ma,Me
      Mars (an angle lord) and Mercury (a trine lord) conjoin. The classical engine of rank: when the ruler of a fortune house (1st, 5th or 9th) joins the ruler of a corner house (1st, 4th, 7th or 10th), status is lifted, and the results ripen in the main periods (dashas) of the two planets involved.
  [anapha] Anapha Yoga  planets=Ve
      Venus occupies the sign before the Moon. Anapha gives a settled constitution and an agreeable presence: health that holds, comfort that is enjoyed rather than chased, and a good name that arrives without campaigning for it. A pull toward simplicity often shows in the second half of life.

--- personality ---
  headline: Taurus rising, Scorpio Moon in Anuradha, Libra Sun — a personality led by Jupiter and tested through Sun.
  strongest=Ju weakest=Su
  == Your rising sign — how you come across
     [1] Your rising sign (the 'Lagna' — the sign that was coming up over the eastern horizon at the moment you were born) is Taurus, at 8°01'. The rising sign describes how you come across, the body you live in, and the way you start things. With Taurus rising, the outward temperament is steady, sensual and immovable once settled, built for accumulation and endurance rather than speed. Comfort is sought sincerely and change resisted instinctively.
     [2] The rising degree also falls in a nakshatra — one of the 27 star-groups the zodiac is divided into, each with its own character — called Krittika, in its 4th quarter. That nakshatra colours the body and the first impression you make: sharp, purifying fire; cuts through falsehood, ambitious and critical.
     [3] Every sign has a ruling planet, and the ruler of your rising sign — Venus — is the planet that stands for you personally in the chart. It sits in your 6th house in Libra, which is its own sign, so it is on home ground; it is also retrograde, which turns its energy inward. Its overall strength (a combined score from its sign, house, aspects and other factors) comes to 46 out of 100 (moderate). Because it sits in the 6th, the centre of gravity of your life leans toward health: that is the agenda that colours everything you attempt, and your sense of who you are is bound up in it.
     [4] Venus itself falls in the nakshatra Vishakha, in its 2nd quarter, whose ruling planet is Jupiter. The flavour of that nakshatra is: goal-fixated determination; twin ambitions pursued with fervour. Jupiter, the nakshatra's ruler, is neutral toward Venus, so this quality comes through plainly — neither boosted nor blocked.
     [5] For Taurus rising, Saturn is the textbook yogakaraka — it rules both your 9th house of fortune and your 10th house of career, so its strong periods build career and fortune together. Jupiter (your 8th and 11th) and the Moon (your 3rd) work against the grain for this rising sign. Mars, as ruler of your 7th, is one of the two planets the tradition watches for health in its periods.
  == Your Moon — the mind and its moods
     [1] In Vedic astrology the Moon stands for the mind: your moods, your instincts, what you need in order to feel safe. Your Moon is in Scorpio in your 7th house, which is the sign where it is debilitated — its weakest position; its overall strength comes to 21 out of 100 (afflicted). With the Moon in Scorpio, the mind runs deep, secretive and intense, with feelings neither displayed nor discarded. This is the Moon's hardest station and the compensation is formidable psychological depth.
     [2] The Moon falls in the nakshatra Anuradha, in its 2nd quarter, whose ruling planet is Saturn. The flavour of that nakshatra is: loyal, disciplined devotion; friendship as a spiritual force. This is the single most personal signature in your chart: it sets the sequence of your planetary periods (the Vimshottari dasha, the timing system used throughout this app), and it describes how you actually experience your own life from the inside. Saturn, the nakshatra's ruler, is neutral toward Moon, so this quality comes through plainly — neither boosted nor blocked.
     [3] Because the Moon sits in your 7th house, your emotional life revolves around marriage. That is where you go for comfort, and it is also where you are most easily unsettled.
  == Your Sun — the will and the core self
     [1] The Sun stands for your will, your sense of self, and the things you need to be respected for. Your Sun is in Libra in your 6th house, which is the sign where it is debilitated — its weakest position; its overall strength comes to 16 out of 100 (afflicted). With the Sun in Libra, the will is relational and consultative: your identity is negotiated rather than asserted, which costs the Sun its natural authority but produces genuine fairness.
     [2] Because the Sun sits in your 6th house, your identity is staked on health. This is the arena in which you need to matter, and a setback here feels like a failure of self rather than of circumstance.
     [3] The Sun falls in the nakshatra Swati, in its 3rd quarter, whose ruling planet is Rahu. The flavour of that nakshatra is: independent, wind-borne flexibility; scatters then finds its own path. Rahu, the nakshatra's ruler, is neutral toward Sun, so this quality comes through plainly — neither boosted nor blocked.
  == Your strongest and weakest planets
     [1] Each planet in your chart has been scored for overall strength out of 100 — a combination of the sign it is in, the house, the aspects it receives, and a few other classical factors. Jupiter is your strongest planet at 67 (strong), sitting in your 3rd house in Cancer. Its qualities are the ones you reach for by default and perform best with under pressure — whatever Jupiter stands for is where you are genuinely, reliably competent. The biggest things in its favour are: Dignity: Exalted (Uchcha); Ashtakavarga 6 bindus in sign; Baladi avastha: Yuva (adult) — full results.
     [2] Sun is your weakest at 16 (afflicted), in your 6th house in Libra. This is the chart's soft flank: whatever Sun stands for is where you tend to over-compensate, avoid, or quietly hand off to other people. The biggest things weighing on it are: Dignity: Debilitated (Neecha); House placement (6); Aspect from Saturn.
     [3] A lot of your personality is the negotiation between these two. Growth, for this chart, looks less like adding to Jupiter — which needs no help — and more like refusing to let Sun's weakness set the terms, because that is precisely the area you are most tempted to route around.
  == Atmakaraka — what this life is for
     [1] Venus is your Atmakaraka — literally the 'soul significator', the planet that has travelled furthest through its sign (24°45'), which the Jaimini school of astrology treats as the planet carrying the deepest purpose of the life. In simple terms: whatever Venus stands for — placed in your 6th house in Libra — is the lesson this life keeps returning to, in relationships, in work, everywhere. It is less "what you do" and more "what you cannot avoid becoming good at".
     [2] Its Karakamsa — the sign the Atmakaraka occupies in the Navamsa, the ninth-division chart — is Taurus: the inner room where that lesson is actually worked out. A Taurus Karakamsa colours the soul's work as steady, sensual and immovable once settled, built for accumulation and endurance rather than speed. Comfort is sought sincerely and change resisted instinctively.
  == Navamsa — the inner person
     [1] The Navamsa (D-9) is a second chart derived from your birth chart by dividing each sign into nine parts. Tradition reads it for the inner person and for marriage, and treats it as the test of whether what the birth chart promises actually holds. Its rising sign is Pisces. If the birth chart is how life presents itself, the Navamsa is how you are on the inside once the noise settles — and a Pisces inner nature is impressionable, compassionate and porous to atmosphere, absorbing whatever surrounds you and needing boundaries you are reluctant to build. Imagination and faith are the standing strengths. When the outer sign (Taurus) and inner sign (Pisces) differ in element, people who only know you casually often misread you; those close to you meet the Navamsa.
  == By the classical six-fold measure (Shadbala)
     [1] Shadbala is the full classical strength calculation — six separate measures (position, direction, time of birth, motion, nature and aspects) added together and expressed in units called rupas, with each planet having a minimum it is expected to reach. By that measure Jupiter is your strongest planet at 9.25 rupas (142% of its required minimum) and Venus the weakest at 5.59 rupas (102%). This agrees with the quicker overall score above, which makes the reading more solid.
     [2] Practically: lean on Jupiter's themes when the stakes are high, and give Venus's themes more preparation time than feels necessary.
  == Aspects on your rising sign — who edits the personality
     [1] Planets influence houses they do not sit in by 'aspecting' them — casting their influence across the chart to particular houses. Every planet aspects the 7th house from itself, and Mars, Jupiter, Saturn, Rahu and Ketu each have extra special aspects. The following planets aspect your rising sign, and each one edits the personality others meet:
     [2] Moon aspects your rising sign from your 7th house — the full 7th-house aspect every planet casts.
     [3] Mars aspects your rising sign from your 7th house — the full 7th-house aspect every planet casts — in Mars's case direct and unavoidable.
     [4] Mercury aspects your rising sign from your 7th house — the full 7th-house aspect every planet casts.
     [5] Saturn aspects your rising sign from your 4th house — Saturn's special 10th-house aspect — the aspect of duty imposed and work scrutinised.
     [6] Rahu aspects your rising sign from your 5th house — Rahu's special 9th-house aspect — a distorting influence on inherited fortune and belief.
     [7] Each of these adds its own colour on top of the rising sign itself. If people describe you in ways that do not quite match the rising-sign portrait above, one of these planets is usually why.
  == How you are seen vs how you are (Arudha Lagna)
     [1] The Arudha Lagna is the point in the chart that stands for your public image — how the world perceives you, as distinct from how you actually are. Yours falls in Pisces, while your actual rising sign is Taurus. So the public image runs on Pisces's wavelength — impressionable, compassionate and porous to atmosphere, absorbing whatever surrounds you and needing boundaries you are reluctant to build — while the person underneath operates as Taurus. The gap is not dishonesty; it is simply that your reputation forms around the themes of your 11th house. Knowing which conversations are about the image and which are about you is a lifelong advantage.
  == Yoga signatures in the personality
     [1] A 'yoga' is a named combination of planets that the classical texts single out as producing a specific result. 4 combinations in your chart stamp themselves directly on the character: Chandra-Mangala Yoga, Ruchaka Yoga (Pancha Mahapurusha), Yogakaraka in Strength, Anapha Yoga.
     [2] Chandra-Mangala Yoga — Moon and Mars conjoin — an earning combination: entrepreneurial drive fused with instinct. Wealth through self-effort, with a temper that needs channels, not suppression.
     [3] Ruchaka Yoga (Pancha Mahapurusha) — Mars is in its own sign and occupies the 7th house, one of the four corner houses (a 'kendra') — one of the five Mahapurusha ("great person") combinations. It stamps the whole personality with a commanding, athletic physicality and fearless executive nerve. You lead from the front, thrive in competition, defence, surgery or engineering, and carry authority that is taken rather than granted. Anger is the tax on the gift.
     [4] Yogakaraka in Strength — Saturn, the most helpful planet for your rising sign (the 'yogakaraka' — it rules a corner house and a fortunate house at once), occupies the 4th house. A single-planet engine of rank: its main periods (dashas) carry the chart's biggest promotions in status and material standing.
     [5] Anapha Yoga — Venus occupies the sign before the Moon. Anapha gives a settled constitution and an agreeable presence: health that holds, comfort that is enjoyed rather than chased, and a good name that arrives without campaigning for it. A pull toward simplicity often shows in the second half of life.
     [6] Beneath that sits a resilience signature — Neechabhanga Raja Yoga, Vipareeta Raja Yoga (Harsha) — the combinations that convert this chart's difficulties into capability rather than damage. They rarely show early; they are what you are left holding after the hard decade.
     [7] Neechabhanga Raja Yoga — Sun's debilitation is cancelled because Saturn, exalted in that very sign's field, occupies a kendra; and the lord of its exaltation sign, Mars, occupies a kendra from Lagna. What begins as the chart's weakest point matures into a source of unusual strength, typically after early-life struggle in that portfolio.
     [8] Neechabhanga Raja Yoga — Moon's debilitation is cancelled because its dispositor Mars occupies a kendra from Lagna; and its dispositor stands in a kendra from the Moon. What begins as the chart's weakest point matures into a source of unusual strength, typically after early-life struggle in that portfolio.
     [9] Vipareeta Raja Yoga (Harsha) — Venus, lord of the 6th, sits in the 6th — the ruler of a difficult house (the 6th, 8th and 12th are the "dusthana" houses) sitting in another difficult house, which the tradition reads as a reversal. Adversity turns on itself: rivals self-destruct, crises resolve in your favour, and gains arrive through difficulty others cannot stomach.
  == Which planets work for you, and which against
     [1] Which planets help you and which hinder you is not fixed — it depends on your rising sign, because the rising sign decides which houses each planet rules. A planet that rules good houses for Taurus rising works in your favour whatever its natural reputation; one that rules difficult houses works against you even if it is a 'good' planet in general.
     [2] Saturn is the yogakaraka for Taurus rising — the single most helpful planet your rising sign can have, because it rules one of the corner houses and one of the fortunate houses at the same time. Saturn — in your 4th house, in a sign neutral to it, strength 57 out of 100 (moderate). Its condition is the best single predictor of how far your personality converts into standing in the world.
     [3] Working for you: Saturn — in your 4th house, in a sign neutral to it, strength 57 out of 100 (moderate); Mercury — in your 7th house, in an enemy's sign, strength 29 out of 100 (afflicted); Sun — in your 6th house, where it is debilitated, strength 16 out of 100 (afflicted). The traits these planets govern are the ones you can lean on without second-guessing, and their main periods (dashas) feel like the personality working as designed.
     [4] Working against you: Moon — in your 7th house, where it is debilitated, strength 21 out of 100 (afflicted); Jupiter — in your 3rd house, where it is exalted, strength 67 out of 100 (strong); Mars — in your 7th house, in its own sign, strength 56 out of 100 (moderate). These are not evil planets — they are the ones whose house rulerships pull against your rising sign's interests. Where one of them is strong, you have a talent that repeatedly gets you into trouble; where weak, a blind spot you rarely notice.
     [5] Neutral: Venus — in your 6th house, in its own sign, strength 46 out of 100 (moderate). These take their colour from whatever they sit with rather than carrying an agenda of their own.

--- houses ---
  == 1st House — Taurus  occupied=false lord=Ve planets=[] aspects=[Mo:7,Ma:7,Me:7,Sa:10,Ra:9]
     [1] Your 1st house falls in Taurus. This is the part of the chart that covers self, body, vitality, temperament and the overall direction of life. No planet sits in it. That is not a weakness in itself — most houses in most charts are empty. An empty house is read the way the classical texts prescribe: by the condition of the planet that rules it, and by the planets that aspect it from elsewhere.
     [2] Taurus is ruled by Venus, so Venus is the ruler of your 1st house — the planet whose condition decides how this area of life goes. Venus sits in your 6th house at 24°45' Libra, which is its own sign, so it is on home ground. It is also retrograde (appearing to move backwards in the sky at your birth). Counting from the 1st house itself, Venus stands 6 places along — the 6th from its own house, which is one of the three difficult positions (the 6th, 8th and 12th, called 'dusthana'). The matters of this house are undermined a little from within, and putting them right takes deliberate, sustained effort rather than luck. Because the ruler sits in the 6th, the affairs of your 1st house get worked out mostly through health — that is where they actually play out in daily life. Its overall strength — a combined score from its sign, house, aspects and other factors — comes to 46 out of 100 (moderate). Venus can carry the 1st house, but results here follow your own effort closely rather than being handed to you.
     [3] The ruler, Venus, also falls in the star-group (nakshatra) called Vishakha, in its 2nd quarter. Each nakshatra has its own ruling planet and its own flavour — here the ruler is Jupiter, and the flavour is: goal-fixated determination; twin ambitions pursued with fervour. Jupiter, the ruler of that nakshatra, is neutral toward Venus, so this flavour comes through as it is — neither boosted nor blocked.
     [4] Moon aspects this house — that is, it reaches into it from where it sits, the 7th house in Scorpio, where it is debilitated. By nature Moon is a gentle planet (a 'benefic'), and for your rising sign it is a planet whose house rulerships pull against your interests, whatever its natural character (a 'functional malefic'). The aspect landing here is the full 7th-house aspect every planet casts. The Moon's gaze on the Lagna gives an approachable, responsive temperament and a face people find easy to trust; the body and the mood, however, run on tides rather than on a schedule. The good intent is there, but the planet itself is poorly placed, so the protection is real yet thin, and tends to arrive later than you expect. Note the split, though: gentle by nature but working against your rising sign by the houses it rules, so its blessing here comes with strings attached.
     [5] Mars also aspects this house, from the 7th house in Scorpio, in its own sign — a hard planet by nature, and one that pulls against your interests by the houses it rules. The aspect landing here is the full 7th-house aspect every planet casts — in Mars's case direct and unavoidable. Mars's drishti on the Lagna gives physical courage, a competitive edge and a body built for exertion, along with a temper that arrives before the reasoning does. Scars, literal and otherwise, are collected early. A hard planet in a strong position does not merely trouble a house — it also forces you to become competent in its affairs, charging the discipline as its price.
     [6] Mercury also aspects this house, from the 7th house in Scorpio, in an enemy's sign — a hard planet by nature, and one that works in your favour by the houses it rules. The aspect landing here is the full 7th-house aspect every planet casts. Mercury's gaze on the Lagna gives a youthful, quick-witted presence and a nervous system that runs on information; you are articulate long before you are settled. Adaptability is the strength, dispersion the risk. A hard planet in a weak position troubles the house without building much capability in return: the friction comes with little compensating discipline. Its rulerships soften this considerably — a hard planet that works for your rising sign behaves far better than its reputation.
     [7] Saturn also aspects this house, from the 4th house in Leo, in a sign neutral to it — a hard planet by nature, and your rising sign's most helpful planet (the yogakaraka). The aspect landing here is Saturn's special 10th-house aspect — the aspect of duty imposed and work scrutinised. Saturn's drishti on the Lagna sobers the temperament and slows the body's early flowering; you seem older than your years and come into your own late. What is built on this foundation does not fall down. Coming from a hard planet in an ordinary condition, the aspect keeps this house honest — pressure you learn to work with rather than damage. And because this is the most helpful planet your rising sign has (the yogakaraka), its aspect is one of the best structural supports in your chart wherever it lands.
     [8] Rahu also aspects this house, from the 5th house in Virgo, in a great friend's sign, retrograde — a hard planet by nature, and neutral by rulership. The aspect landing here is Rahu's special 9th-house aspect — a distorting influence on inherited fortune and belief. Rahu's drishti on the Lagna amplifies the persona and blurs its edges: unusual magnetism, an unconventional self-presentation, and an identity assembled rather than inherited. Other people's projections stick to you easily. A hard planet in a strong position does not merely trouble a house — it also forces you to become competent in its affairs, charging the discipline as its price.
     [9] The house receives 1 gentle and 4 hard aspects — a contested area whose results swing depending on which of these planets is running its main period (dasha) at the time.
     [10] In short: with no planet in it to speak for it, how your 1st house goes rests on Venus's condition in the 6th and on the aspects described above. Anything that strengthens Venus — its main periods, its transits, deliberate attention to what it stands for — improves this house; nothing else reaches it directly.
  == 2nd House — Gemini  occupied=false lord=Me planets=[] aspects=[Ma:8]
     [1] Your 2nd house falls in Gemini. This is the part of the chart that covers wealth, family, speech, food and accumulated resources. No planet sits in it. That is not a weakness in itself — most houses in most charts are empty. An empty house is read the way the classical texts prescribe: by the condition of the planet that rules it, and by the planets that aspect it from elsewhere.
     [2] Gemini is ruled by Mercury, so Mercury is the ruler of your 2nd house — the planet whose condition decides how this area of life goes. Mercury sits in your 7th house at 5°13' Scorpio, which is an enemy's sign, where it meets resistance. Counting from the 2nd house itself, Mercury stands 6 places along — the 6th from its own house, which is one of the three difficult positions (the 6th, 8th and 12th, called 'dusthana'). The matters of this house are undermined a little from within, and putting them right takes deliberate, sustained effort rather than luck. Because the ruler sits in the 7th, the affairs of your 2nd house get worked out mostly through marriage — that is where they actually play out in daily life. Its overall strength — a combined score from its sign, house, aspects and other factors — comes to 29 out of 100 (afflicted), which is low. The 2nd house is under-resourced at its ruler, and its affairs need conscious support from you before they hold weight.
     [3] The ruler, Mercury, also falls in the star-group (nakshatra) called Anuradha, in its 1st quarter. Each nakshatra has its own ruling planet and its own flavour — here the ruler is Saturn, and the flavour is: loyal, disciplined devotion; friendship as a spiritual force. Saturn, the ruler of that nakshatra, is neutral toward Mercury, so this flavour comes through as it is — neither boosted nor blocked.
     [4] Mars aspects this house — that is, it reaches into it from where it sits, the 7th house in Scorpio, in its own sign. By nature Mars is a hard planet (a 'malefic'), and for your rising sign it is a planet whose house rulerships pull against your interests, whatever its natural character (a 'functional malefic'). The aspect landing here is Mars's special 8th-house aspect — the sudden, wounding one that arrives without notice. Mars's aspect on the 2nd sharpens speech into a weapon and makes wealth something to be fought for and defended rather than inherited. Family relations carry friction; the tongue needs a guard rail. A hard planet in a strong position does not merely trouble a house — it also forces you to become competent in its affairs, charging the discipline as its price.
     [5] Every aspect reaching this house comes from a hard planet. With no gentle planet to balance them, what this house stands for needs conscious defending, and the house tends to mature through difficulty rather than ease — which also means it usually matures well.
     [6] In short: with no planet in it to speak for it, how your 2nd house goes rests on Mercury's condition in the 7th and on the aspects described above. Anything that strengthens Mercury — its main periods, its transits, deliberate attention to what it stands for — improves this house; nothing else reaches it directly.
  == 3rd House — Cancer  occupied=true lord=Mo planets=[Ju] aspects=[Ke:5]
     [1] Your 3rd house falls in Cancer. This is the part of the chart that covers courage, siblings, communication, skills and self-effort. Jupiter sits in it, so the affairs of this house are things you live directly rather than watch from a distance.
     [2] Cancer is ruled by Moon, so Moon is the ruler of your 3rd house — the planet whose condition decides how this area of life goes. Moon sits in your 7th house at 6°48' Scorpio, which is the sign where it is debilitated — its weakest position. Counting from the 3rd house itself, Moon stands 5 places along — the 5th from its own house, which is one of the supportive positions (the corner houses 1, 4, 7, 10 and the fortunate houses 5 and 9). The ruler actively backs what it rules. Because the ruler sits in the 7th, the affairs of your 3rd house get worked out mostly through marriage — that is where they actually play out in daily life. Its overall strength — a combined score from its sign, house, aspects and other factors — comes to 21 out of 100 (afflicted), which is low. The 3rd house is under-resourced at its ruler, and its affairs need conscious support from you before they hold weight.
     [3] The ruler, Moon, also falls in the star-group (nakshatra) called Anuradha, in its 2nd quarter. Each nakshatra has its own ruling planet and its own flavour — here the ruler is Saturn, and the flavour is: loyal, disciplined devotion; friendship as a spiritual force. Saturn, the ruler of that nakshatra, is neutral toward Moon, so this flavour comes through as it is — neither boosted nor blocked.
     [4] Jupiter sits here at 14°37' Cancer. For your Taurus rising sign, Jupiter rules your 8th and 11th houses — longevity and gains — which makes it a planet whose house rulerships pull against your interests, whatever its natural character (a 'functional malefic'). Jupiter in the 3rd gives wise initiative — courage tempered by ethics. Siblings prosper or are themselves guru-figures. Writing and teaching flow naturally, though ambition here is softer than talent; effort must be deliberate. Cancer is the sign where Jupiter is exalted — its strongest possible position. Everything Jupiter stands for runs at full, sometimes overwhelming, capacity here; this is one of the power centres of your chart.
     [5] Jupiter falls in the nakshatra Pushya (4th quarter), ruled by Saturn, whose flavour is: nourishing, dutiful, deeply auspicious; grows what it protects. Saturn, the ruler of that nakshatra, is neutral toward Jupiter, so this flavour comes through as it is — neither boosted nor blocked.
     [6] Jupiter and the house ruler Moon both sit in nakshatras ruled by Saturn. They are wired to the same source: what they stand for rises and falls together, and Saturn's own main period (dasha) switches this house on twice over.
     [7] Ketu aspects this house — that is, it reaches into it from where it sits, the 11th house in Pisces, in an enemy's sign, retrograde. By nature Ketu is a hard planet (a 'malefic'), and for your rising sign it is neither for you nor against you by rulership (functionally neutral). The aspect landing here is Ketu's special 5th-house aspect — a dissolving touch on intelligence and children. Ketu's glance on the 3rd makes courage sporadic but instinctive: you act decisively in flashes and disengage between them. Skills are picked up intuitively; sibling ties are karmically loose. A hard planet in a weak position troubles the house without building much capability in return: the friction comes with little compensating discipline.
     [8] Every aspect reaching this house comes from a hard planet. With no gentle planet to balance them, what this house stands for needs conscious defending, and the house tends to mature through difficulty rather than ease — which also means it usually matures well.

The 4th house is under-resourced at its ruler, and its affairs need conscious support from you before they hold weight.
     [3] The ruler, Sun, also falls in the star-group (nakshatra) called Swati, in its 3rd quarter. Each nakshatra has its own ruling planet and its own flavour — here the ruler is Rahu, and the flavour is: independent, wind-borne flexibility; scatters then finds its own path. Rahu, the ruler of that nakshatra, is neutral toward Sun, so this flavour comes through as it is — neither boosted nor blocked.
     [4] Saturn sits here at 17°59' Leo. For your Taurus rising sign, Saturn rules your 9th and 10th houses — fortune and career — which makes it the single most helpful planet your rising sign can have (the 'yogakaraka'), because it rules one of the supporting houses and one of the fortunate houses at the same time. Saturn in the 4th chills the hearth: emotional reserve at home, a mother marked by duty or hardship, property acquired late or through labour. Inner peace is a discipline, not a default — and once practised, unshakeable. Leo is neutral ground for Saturn — the sign neither helps nor hinders it. What you get out of this house tracks what you put in almost exactly, with no subsidy and no surcharge.
     [5] Saturn falls in the nakshatra Purva Phalguni (2nd quarter), ruled by Venus, whose flavour is: pleasure-loving warmth; charm, patronage and creative enjoyment. Venus, the ruler of that nakshatra, is a natural friend of Saturn, so this flavour is passed on willingly and arrives in a form you recognise.
     [6] No planet aspects the 4th house. Its affairs run on the ruler's condition alone, with nothing from outside either reinforcing or interfering — outcomes here are quieter and more in your own hands than elsewhere in the chart.
  == 5th House — Virgo  occupied=true lord=Me planets=[Ra] aspects=[Ke:7]
     [1] Your 5th house falls in Virgo. This is the part of the chart that covers intelligence, children, creativity, romance and purva-punya (past merit). Rahu sits in it, so the affairs of this house are things you live directly rather than watch from a distance.
     [2] Virgo is ruled by Mercury, so Mercury is the ruler of your 5th house — the planet whose condition decides how this area of life goes. Mercury sits in your 7th house at 5°13' Scorpio, which is an enemy's sign, where it meets resistance. Counting from the 5th house itself, Mercury stands 3 places along — the 3rd from its own house, a workable but unremarkable position that neither helps nor hurts. Because the ruler sits in the 7th, the affairs of your 5th house get worked out mostly through marriage — that is where they actually play out in daily life. Its overall strength — a combined score from its sign, house, aspects and other factors — comes to 29 out of 100 (afflicted), which is low. The 5th house is under-resourced at its ruler, and its affairs need conscious support from you before they hold weight.
     [3] The ruler, Mercury, also falls in the star-group (nakshatra) called Anuradha, in its 1st quarter. Each nakshatra has its own ruling planet and its own flavour — here the ruler is Saturn, and the flavour is: loyal, disciplined devotion; friendship as a spiritual force. Saturn, the ruler of that nakshatra, is neutral toward Mercury, so this flavour comes through as it is — neither boosted nor blocked.
     [4] Rahu sits here at 0°49' Virgo. Rahu rules no sign of its own. It works through the planet that owns the sign it sits in, through the ruler of its star-group (nakshatra), and through whatever it sits beside — a shadow that takes on the colour of its surroundings rather than a house ruler with an agenda of its own. Rahu in the 5th intoxicates intellect and romance: unconventional love, gifted but complex children, speculative genius that must be caged in rules. Political instinct is strong. Mantra practice tames what analysis cannot. Virgo belongs to a great friend of Rahu, so it is warmly hosted here. Its promises are delivered with goodwill, and usually with a little extra. Rahu is retrograde — it appeared to be moving backwards through the sky when you were born. In practice that turns its energy inward and intensifies it: the themes of this house come back around more than once, and matters tend to conclude on the second or third pass rather than the first. One refinement: houses can be counted two ways. By whole signs (used above) Rahu is in the 5th, but by the more precise method that measures from the exact house boundaries (Bhava Chalit), it falls in the 4th. Read its concrete, day-to-day results in the 4th house too; its strength and comfort by sign, described above, stay as they are.
     [5] Rahu falls in the nakshatra Uttara Phalguni (2nd quarter), ruled by Sun, whose flavour is: contractual grace; balances generosity with prudent alliance. Sun, the ruler of that nakshatra, is a natural enemy of Rahu, so even a well-placed Rahu has its results filtered through an unsympathetic hand — expect what it promises to arrive altered, later, or at a price.
     [6] Ketu aspects this house — that is, it reaches into it from where it sits, the 11th house in Pisces, in an enemy's sign, retrograde. By nature Ketu is a hard planet (a 'malefic'), and for your rising sign it is neither for you nor against you by rulership (functionally neutral). The aspect landing here is the full 7th-house aspect every planet casts — in Ketu's case direct detachment. Ketu's glance on the 5th gives intuitive, non-linear intelligence and a spiritual bent to creativity, alongside karmic complications around children or a romance that dissolves rather than concludes. Mantra and technical study both come easily. A hard planet in a weak position troubles the house without building much capability in return: the friction comes with little compensating discipline.
     [7] Every aspect reaching this house comes from a hard planet. With no gentle planet to balance them, what this house stands for needs conscious defending, and the house tends to mature through difficulty rather than ease — which also means it usually matures well.
  == 6th House — Libra  occupied=true lord=Ve planets=[Su,Ve] aspects=[Sa:3]
     [1] Your 6th house falls in Libra. This is the part of the chart that covers health, debts, enemies, service, litigation and daily discipline. Sun, Venus all sit in it, which makes this one of the main arenas of your life — a lot happens here, and it happens to you directly.

     [4] Sun sits here at 16°05' Libra. Employment under others chafes until authority is earned. Libra is the sign where Sun is debilitated — its weakest position in the zodiac.      [5] Sun falls in the nakshatra Swati (3rd quarter), ruled by Rahu, whose flavour is: independent, wind-borne flexibility; scatters then finds its own path. Rahu, the ruler of that nakshatra, is neutral toward Sun, so this flavour comes through as it is — neither boosted nor blocked.
     [6] Venus sits here at 24°45' Libra. Sugar, kidneys and hormonal balance are the health ledger. Libra is Venus's own sign, so it is on home ground here: what it gives is stable and self-sufficient, and does not depend on other planets cooperating. Venus is retrograde — it appeared to be moving backwards through the sky when you were born. In practice that turns its energy inward and intensifies it: the themes of this house come back around more than once, and matters tend to conclude on the second or third pass rather than the first.
     [7] Venus falls in the nakshatra Vishakha (2nd quarter), ruled by Jupiter, whose flavour is: goal-fixated determination; twin ambitions pursued with fervour. Jupiter, the ruler of that nakshatra, is neutral toward Venus, so this flavour comes through as it is — neither boosted nor blocked.
     [8] Saturn aspects this house — that is, it reaches into it from where it sits, the 4th house in Leo, in a sign neutral to it. Chronic rather than acute ailments are the health signature. And because this is the most helpful planet your rising sign has (the yogakaraka), its aspect is one of the best structural supports in your chart wherever it lands.
     [9] Every aspect reaching this house comes from a hard planet. With no gentle planet to balance them, what this house stands for needs conscious defending, and the house tends to mature through difficulty rather than ease — which also means it usually matures well.
     [10] Sun–Venus pairs sovereign and artist — creative authority, refined presence, romance complicated by pride.   == 8th House — Sagittarius  occupied=false lord=Ju planets=[] aspects=[]
     [1] Your 8th house falls in Sagittarius. This is the part of the chart that covers longevity, transformation, inheritance, occult depth and sudden events. No planet sits in it. That is not a weakness in itself — most houses in most charts are empty. An empty house is read the way the classical texts prescribe: by the condition of the planet that rules it, and by the planets that aspect it from elsewhere.
     [2] Sagittarius is ruled by Jupiter, so Jupiter is the ruler of your 8th house — the planet whose condition decides how this area of life goes. Jupiter sits in your 3rd house at 14°37' Cancer, which is the sign where it is exalted — at its very strongest. Counting from the 8th house itself, Jupiter stands 8 places along — the 8th from its own house, which is one of the three difficult positions (the 6th, 8th and 12th, called 'dusthana'). The matters of this house are undermined a little from within, and putting them right takes deliberate, sustained effort rather than luck. Because the ruler sits in the 3rd, the affairs of your 8th house get worked out mostly through courage — that is where they actually play out in daily life. Its overall strength — a combined score from its sign, house, aspects and other factors — comes to 67 out of 100 (strong), so Jupiter is in a position to actually deliver what the 8th house stands for, not merely promise it.
     [3] The ruler, Jupiter, also falls in the star-group (nakshatra) called Pushya, in its 4th quarter. Each nakshatra has its own ruling planet and its own flavour — here the ruler is Saturn, and the flavour is: nourishing, dutiful, deeply auspicious; grows what it protects. Saturn, the ruler of that nakshatra, is neutral toward Jupiter, so this flavour comes through as it is — neither boosted nor blocked.
     [4] No planet aspects the 8th house. Its affairs run on the ruler's condition alone, with nothing from outside either reinforcing or interfering — outcomes here are quieter and more in your own hands than elsewhere in the chart.
     [5] In short: with no planet in it to speak for it, how your 8th house goes rests on Jupiter's condition in the 3rd and on the aspects described above. Anything that strengthens Jupiter — its main periods, its transits, deliberate attention to what it stands for — improves this house; nothing else reaches it directly.

It works through the planet that owns the sign it sits in, through the ruler of its star-group (nakshatra), and through whatever it sits beside — a shadow that takes on the colour of its surroundings rather than a house ruler with an agenda of its own. Ketu in the 11th prunes the network: few friends, strange gains, desires that extinguish upon examination. Income arrives through niche or technical channels. The social ladder holds no rungs you care to grip — freedom is the actual profit. Pisces belongs to an enemy of Ketu, so it meets resistance here — a guest whose host does not much like it. Results in this house take roughly twice the usual persistence to arrive. Ketu is retrograde — it appeared to be moving backwards through the sky when you were born. In practice that turns its energy inward and intensifies it: the themes of this house come back around more than once, and matters tend to conclude on the second or third pass rather than the first. One refinement: houses can be counted two ways. By whole signs (used above) Ketu is in the 11th, but by the more precise method that measures from the exact house boundaries (Bhava Chalit), it falls in the 10th. Read its concrete, day-to-day results in the 10th house too; its strength and comfort by sign, described above, stay as they are.
     [5] Ketu falls in the nakshatra Purva Bhadrapada (4th quarter), ruled by Jupiter, whose flavour is: fiery twin-front transformation; intensity behind an austere face. Jupiter, the ruler of that nakshatra, is neutral toward Ketu, so this flavour comes through as it is — neither boosted nor blocked.
     [6] Jupiter aspects this house — that is, it reaches into it from where it sits, the 3rd house in Cancer, where it is exalted. By nature Jupiter is a gentle planet (a 'benefic'), and for your rising sign it is a planet whose house rulerships pull against your interests, whatever its natural character (a 'functional malefic'). The aspect landing here is Jupiter's special 9th-house aspect — its blessing on fortune and right living, and the most benevolent aspect there is. Jupiter's glance on the 11th expands gains and fills the network with people of standing; income grows steadily and desires are largely fulfilled. The risk is a portfolio inflated by optimism. Because this gentle planet is itself well placed, its aspect is a genuine protection — something you can rely on when this house is tested. Note the split, though: gentle by nature but working against your rising sign by the houses it rules, so its blessing here comes with strings attached.
     [7] Rahu also aspects this house, from the 5th house in Virgo, in a great friend's sign, retrograde — a hard planet by nature, and neutral by rulership. The aspect landing here is the full 7th-house aspect every planet casts — in Rahu's case direct and obsessive. Rahu's aspect on the 11th is classically excellent for gains — large, unusual, foreign-sourced income and a network far beyond your origins. Desires, however, refill as fast as they are met. A hard planet in a strong position does not merely trouble a house — it also forces you to become competent in its affairs, charging the discipline as its price.
     [8] The house receives 1 gentle and 1 hard aspect — a contested area whose results swing depending on which of these planets is running its main period (dasha) at the time.
  == 12th House — Aries  occupied=false lord=Ma planets=[] aspects=[Su:7,Ve:7]
     [1] Your 12th house falls in Aries. This is the part of the chart that covers expenditure, losses, foreign lands, isolation, sleep and moksha. No planet sits in it. That is not a weakness in itself — most houses in most charts are empty. An empty house is read the way the classical texts prescribe: by the condition of the planet that rules it, and by the planets that aspect it from elsewhere.
     [2] Aries is ruled by Mars, so Mars is the ruler of your 12th house — the planet whose condition decides how this area of life goes. Mars sits in your 7th house at 6°43' Scorpio, which is its own sign, so it is on home ground. Counting from the 12th house itself, Mars stands 8 places along — the 8th from its own house, which is one of the three difficult positions (the 6th, 8th and 12th, called 'dusthana'). The matters of this house are undermined a little from within, and putting them right takes deliberate, sustained effort rather than luck. Because the ruler sits in the 7th, the affairs of your 12th house get worked out mostly through marriage — that is where they actually play out in daily life. Its overall strength — a combined score from its sign, house, aspects and other factors — comes to 56 out of 100 (moderate). Mars can carry the 12th house, but results here follow your own effort closely rather than being handed to you.
     [3] The ruler, Mars, also falls in the star-group (nakshatra) called Anuradha, in its 2nd quarter. Each nakshatra has its own ruling planet and its own flavour — here the ruler is Saturn, and the flavour is: loyal, disciplined devotion; friendship as a spiritual force. Saturn, the ruler of that nakshatra, is neutral toward Mars, so this flavour comes through as it is — neither boosted nor blocked.
     [4] Sun aspects this house — that is, it reaches into it from where it sits, the 6th house in Libra, where it is debilitated. By nature Sun is a hard planet (a 'malefic'), and for your rising sign it is a planet that works in your favour by the houses it rules (a 'functional benefic'). The aspect landing here is the full 7th-house aspect every planet casts. The Sun's gaze on the 12th spends the ego's fuel on distant, private or institutional ends — foreign work, seclusion, hospitals, or genuine spiritual withdrawal. Expenditure is dignified rather than wasteful, but vitality and sleep need guarding. A hard planet in a weak position troubles the house without building much capability in return: the friction comes with little compensating discipline. Its rulerships soften this considerably — a hard planet that works for your rising sign behaves far better than its reputation.
     [5] Venus also aspects this house, from the 6th house in Libra, in its own sign, retrograde — a gentle planet by nature, and neutral by rulership. The aspect landing here is the full 7th-house aspect every planet casts. Venus's aspect on the 12th spends on comfort, beauty and private pleasures, and gives the classical blessing on everything the 12th stands for — bed comforts, foreign residence, and the surrender that mature devotion requires. Discretion is the discipline. Because this gentle planet is itself well placed, its aspect is a genuine protection — something you can rely on when this house is tested.
     [6] The house receives 1 gentle and 1 hard aspect — a contested area whose results swing depending on which of these planets is running its main period (dasha) at the time.
     [7] In short: with no planet in it to speak for it, how your 12th house goes rests on Mars's condition in the 7th and on the aspects described above. Anything that strengthens Mars — its main periods, its transits, deliberate attention to what it stands for — improves this house; nothing else reaches it directly.

  == The marriage houses and significators
     [1] Marriage is read from your 7th house and from the planets that naturally stand for partnership. Below are the two deeper views the tradition adds to that: the marriage pada, which describes the institution, and the Navamsa, which describes the daily life inside it.
     [2] Your Upapada Lagna (the marriage pada — the arudha of the 12th house) falls in Gemini, and Mercury rules it, sitting in your 7th house, in an enemy's sign. This point describes marriage as a standing institution in your life rather than as romance — the household, the in-laws, the public fact of being married. Its condition is the best single indicator of how settled and respected that arrangement tends to feel from the inside.
     [3] Your Navamsa (the D-9 divisional chart of marriage and inner strength) is where married life is actually read, and it says this: the Navamsa rising sign is Pisces, ruled by Jupiter; its 7th house falls in Virgo, with Moon, Mars, Saturn there; and Venus lands in Taurus. Treat this as the private texture of the marriage — what the two of you are like at home on an ordinary Tuesday, rather than what the relationship looks like to everyone else.

  == Mangal Dosha
     [1] Mars stands in the 7th from your rising sign, and the 1st from your Moon, which technically forms Mangal Dosha — but your chart cancels it: Mars stands in its own sign (Scorpio) — a dignified Mars is held not to afflict; Jupiter aspects Mars — the classical benefic override. That means the heat is there without the affliction the tradition warns about. In daily life it reads as directness rather than damage: you say the difficult thing early, which is usually kinder than the alternative.
  == What your partner tends to be like
     [1] Because Mars rules your 7th house, the person you marry tends to carry that planet's nature — you are drawn to it, and you recognise it quickly. Tradition also reads a direction from this, South of your birthplace, but hold that one loosely: direction rules are the softest technique on this page.
     [2] There are no strong foreign markers on your marriage axis, which leans the reading toward a partner from a familiar cultural circle. That is a tendency in the chart, not a boundary on your life — plenty of these charts marry across every kind of distance.
     [3] Mercury, as your spouse significator, colours their temperament more than anything else here. Look for a strong Mercury signature in whom you actually choose — it is usually more visible in how they behave under stress than in how they present at first.
  == What tradition offers, and what actually helps
     [1] The standard literature recommends strengthening Venus in the traditional way — Friday observances, white clothing, courtesy toward your partner. The last of those is the one that carries the weight: attention paid to the relationship is what the practice is really training.

--- section: cautions ---
  headline: 7 areas in your chart deserve conscious handling rather than luck. Each one below comes with something practical to do about it, and none of them is a verdict.

  == How to read this
     [1] These are tendencies, not sentences, and none of them is a verdict on your life. Classical astrology lists them for one reason: so you can steer. That is why every caution below arrives with the thing that actually helps — a caution without a counter-measure is just anxiety with a citation.
     [2] Nothing here is medical, legal or financial advice, and none of it should stand in for a professional who can look at your actual situation.

  == Where these come from
     [1] From your chart: the ruler of your rising sign (Venus), your yogakaraka (Saturn), the ruler of your Moon's nakshatra (Saturn) and your strongest supportive planet (Mercury) are the ones working in your favour. Their numbers, colours, days and directions are what this section calls yours.

     [3] Treat all of this the way tradition actually intends it: as a set of preferences that tilt the odds slightly and cost nothing to follow, not as rules that decide outcomes. Wearing your colour to an interview is worth about as much as the confidence it gives you, which is not nothing.

  == Part 1: Personality
     # The foundation: Taurus Lagna
       Your Lagna is at 8°01' Taurus in Krittika pada 4.
       No planet occupies the 1st house; Moon, Mars, Mercury, Saturn and Rahu aspect it.

       - The shadow side is stubbornness, possessiveness and resisting change past the point it helps. People feel your energy immediately.
       - Physically: a sturdy frame, strong neck and shoulders, a pleasant face and voice; the throat and neck are the sensitive area.
     # Venus, the Lagna lord, in the 6th house (Libra, Vishakha)
       Venus is at 24°45' Libra in Vishakha pada 2, in its own sign, retrograde.
       - You fight your way up: service, competition and problem-solving define you; health and debts need managing, and you beat rivals through persistence.

       - Ketu's aspect gives sudden detachment: you can become intensely involved and then emotionally withdraw.

       - Your Arudha Lagna (public image) is Pisces in the 11th, with Ketu. The world sees you as compassionate, intuitive and spiritual, coloured by detachment & depth.

  == Part 4: Marriage and relationships
     # The 7th house: Scorpio

       - Mercury here: Mercury in the 7th seeks a clever, conversational partner; the marriage is a running dialogue, and its health tracks the quality of that dialogue. Business partnerships, trade and negotiation-heavy careers prosper.
       - Jupiter's aspect: Jupiter's glance on the 7th is the classical protection of marriage: a principled, often wiser or older partner, ethical business alliances, and public dealings conducted in good faith.
       - Ketu's aspect: Ketu's aspect on the 7th introduces detachment into partnership — a spiritually inclined or distant partner, or a marriage you are present for without being attached to.
     # Venus and Jupiter — the marriage significators

       - Jupiter (the husband-karaka for a woman's chart) is exalted in the 3rd at 1.42×, and in a neutral sign in the Navamsa (Scorpio) — a warm, supportive significator.

     # Mangal Dosha: present but cancelled
       - Mars stands in the 7th from your rising sign, and the 1st from your Moon, which technically forms Mangal Dosha — but your chart cancels it: Mars stands in its own sign (Scorpio) — a dignified Mars is held not to afflict; Jupiter aspects Mars — the classical benefic override.
       - That means the heat is there without the affliction the tradition warns about.
     # What your partner tends to be like

       - There are no strong foreign markers on your marriage axis, which leans the reading toward a partner from a familiar cultural circle.
       - That is a tendency in the chart, not a boundary on your life — plenty of these charts marry across every kind of distance.
     # When marriage is most likely
       - No marriage-connected main period stands out in the remaining band; Jupiter's transits over the 7th are the trigger to watch.
     summary: Your marriage houses are well supported — partnership is one of the stronger things in this chart, and the interesting question is when rather than whether.
     summary: Marriage score 70/100 — read it together with the windows: timing matters more than the average for this house.
