--- yogas ---
  [chandra-mangala] Chandra-Mangala Yoga  planets=Mo,Ma
      Moon and Mars conjoin — an earning combination: entrepreneurial drive fused with instinct. Wealth through self-effort, with a temper that needs channels, not suppression.
  [raja-Sa-Ve] Raja Yoga (Kendra–Trikona link)  planets=Ve,Sa
      Venus (an angle lord) and Saturn (a trine lord) stand in mutual aspect. The classical engine of rank: when the ruler of a fortune house (1st, 5th or 9th) joins the ruler of a corner house (1st, 4th, 7th or 10th), status is lifted, and the results ripen in the main periods (dashas) of the two planets involved.
  [raja-Ma-Sa] Raja Yoga (Kendra–Trikona link)  planets=Ma,Sa
      Mars (an angle lord) and Saturn (a trine lord) stand in mutual aspect. The classical engine of rank: when the ruler of a fortune house (1st, 5th or 9th) joins the ruler of a corner house (1st, 4th, 7th or 10th), status is lifted, and the results ripen in the main periods (dashas) of the two planets involved.
  [dhana-5-2] Dhana Yoga (5th–2nd lords)  planets=Sa,Ma
      The 5th lord Saturn stands in mutual aspect with the 2nd lord Mars — fortune's lord touching the wealth axis. Money arrives through merit, timing or backing rather than grind alone.
  [dhana-9-11] Dhana Yoga (9th–11th lords)  planets=Me,Su
      The 9th lord Mercury joins the 11th lord Sun — fortune's lord touching the wealth axis. Money arrives through merit, timing or backing rather than grind alone.
  [parivartana-Ju-Me] Parivartana Yoga (Dainya)  planets=Ju,Me
      Jupiter and Mercury sit in each other's signs — a parivartana, or mutual exchange, linking the 3rd and 6th with the 9th and 12th. An exchange fuses two houses: neither can be read without the other, and whatever happens to one shows up in the other. A difficult house is inside the exchange, so its results arrive the hard way — obstruction first, resolution afterwards. The tradition still counts it a yoga: what it costs early it tends to return as capability later.
  [kemadruma] Kemadruma Yoga (cancelled)  planets=Mo
      The Moon has no planetary neighbour in the signs beside it — Kemadruma forms — but it is cancelled because planets occupy the corner houses counted from the Moon. The isolation shows only as an occasional need for solitude, not as the classical loneliness of the yoga.
  [amala-moon] Amala Yoga  planets=Ve
      Only the gentle planets (Venus) occupy the 10th from the Moon. The "stainless" yoga: reputation stays clean, work is respected, and standing survives controversy. Career fortunes rise on merit rather than manoeuvre.

--- personality ---
  headline: Libra rising, Aquarius Moon in Shatabhisha, Sagittarius Sun — a personality led by Mars and tested through Jupiter.
  strongest=Ma weakest=Ju
  == Your rising sign — how you come across
     [1] Your rising sign (the 'Lagna' — the sign that was coming up over the eastern horizon at the moment you were born) is Libra, at 0°49'. The rising sign describes how you come across, the body you live in, and the way you start things. With Libra rising, the outward temperament is gracious, balancing and relationship-oriented; you think by consulting. Charm is effortless, decisiveness is not.
     [2] The rising degree also falls in a nakshatra — one of the 27 star-groups the zodiac is divided into, each with its own character — called Chitra, in its 3rd quarter. That nakshatra colours the body and the first impression you make: brilliant, architectural vision; polishes form into beauty.
     [3] Every sign has a ruling planet, and the ruler of your rising sign — Venus — is the planet that stands for you personally in the chart. It sits in your 2nd house in Scorpio, which is a friend's sign, where it is comfortable; it is also combust — so close to the Sun that its own light is drowned out. Its overall strength (a combined score from its sign, house, aspects and other factors) comes to 28 out of 100 (afflicted). Because it sits in the 2nd, the centre of gravity of your life leans toward wealth: that is the agenda that colours everything you attempt, and your sense of who you are is bound up in it.
     [4] Venus itself falls in the nakshatra Jyeshtha, in its 4th quarter, whose ruling planet is Mercury. The flavour of that nakshatra is: eldest-born intensity; protective authority won through trials. Mercury, the nakshatra's ruler, is a friend of Venus, so this quality comes through warmly and is easy for others to read.
     [5] For Libra rising, Saturn is the yogakaraka — it rules your 4th house of home and your 5th house of intelligence, giving a patient, structural rise. Mercury, ruling your 9th house of fortune, carries your luck. Jupiter (your 3rd and 6th) is the chief planet working against you; Mars rules your 2nd and 7th, the two houses the tradition watches for health.
  == Your Moon — the mind and its moods
     [1] In Vedic astrology the Moon stands for the mind: your moods, your instincts, what you need in order to feel safe. Your Moon is in Aquarius in your 5th house, which is a friend's sign, where it is comfortable; its overall strength comes to 49 out of 100 (moderate). With the Moon in Aquarius, the mind is detached and observational, feeling at one remove and more comfortable with humanity than with individuals. Emotional independence is real, occasionally to the point of remoteness.
     [2] The Moon falls in the nakshatra Shatabhisha, in its 3rd quarter, whose ruling planet is Rahu. The flavour of that nakshatra is: veiled healing; solitary depths, mysticism and medicine. This is the single most personal signature in your chart: it sets the sequence of your planetary periods (the Vimshottari dasha, the timing system used throughout this app), and it describes how you actually experience your own life from the inside. Rahu, the nakshatra's ruler, is neutral toward Moon, so this quality comes through plainly — neither boosted nor blocked.
     [3] Because the Moon sits in your 5th house, your emotional life revolves around intelligence. That is where you go for comfort, and it is also where you are most easily unsettled.
  == Your Sun — the will and the core self
     [1] The Sun stands for your will, your sense of self, and the things you need to be respected for. Your Sun is in Sagittarius in your 3rd house, which is a sign that is neutral to it — neither helped nor hindered; its overall strength comes to 43 out of 100 (weak). With the Sun in Sagittarius, the will is principled and expansive: your identity rests on beliefs held, defended and taught.
     [2] Because the Sun sits in your 3rd house, your identity is staked on courage. This is the arena in which you need to matter, and a setback here feels like a failure of self rather than of circumstance.
     [3] The Sun falls in the nakshatra Mula, in its 2nd quarter, whose ruling planet is Ketu. The flavour of that nakshatra is: root-cutting inquiry; destroys to find foundational truth. Ketu, the nakshatra's ruler, is neutral toward Sun, so this quality comes through plainly — neither boosted nor blocked.
  == Your strongest and weakest planets
     [1] Each planet in your chart has been scored for overall strength out of 100 — a combination of the sign it is in, the house, the aspects it receives, and a few other classical factors. Mars is your strongest planet at 52 (moderate), sitting in your 5th house in Aquarius. Its qualities are the ones you reach for by default and perform best with under pressure — whatever Mars stands for is where you are genuinely, reliably competent. The biggest things in its favour are: Dignity: Friend's Sign (Mitra); House placement (5); Aspect from Jupiter.
     [2] Jupiter is your weakest at 22 (afflicted), in your 9th house in Gemini. This is the chart's soft flank: whatever Jupiter stands for is where you tend to over-compensate, avoid, or quietly hand off to other people. The biggest things weighing on it are: Dignity: Great Enemy's Sign (Adhi Shatru); Aspect from Sun; Aspect from Mercury.
     [3] A lot of your personality is the negotiation between these two. Growth, for this chart, looks less like adding to Mars — which needs no help — and more like refusing to let Jupiter's weakness set the terms, because that is precisely the area you are most tempted to route around.
  == Atmakaraka — what this life is for
     [1] Venus is your Atmakaraka — literally the 'soul significator', the planet that has travelled furthest through its sign (29°32'), which the Jaimini school of astrology treats as the planet carrying the deepest purpose of the life. In simple terms: whatever Venus stands for — placed in your 2nd house in Scorpio — is the lesson this life keeps returning to, in relationships, in work, everywhere. It is less "what you do" and more "what you cannot avoid becoming good at".
     [2] Its Karakamsa — the sign the Atmakaraka occupies in the Navamsa, the ninth-division chart — is Pisces: the inner room where that lesson is actually worked out. A Pisces Karakamsa colours the soul's work as impressionable, compassionate and porous to atmosphere, absorbing whatever surrounds you and needing boundaries you are reluctant to build. Imagination and faith are the standing strengths.
  == Navamsa — the inner person
     [1] The Navamsa (D-9) is a second chart derived from your birth chart by dividing each sign into nine parts. Tradition reads it for the inner person and for marriage, and treats it as the test of whether what the birth chart promises actually holds. Its rising sign is Libra. If the birth chart is how life presents itself, the Navamsa is how you are on the inside once the noise settles — and a Libra inner nature is gracious, balancing and relationship-oriented; you think by consulting. Charm is effortless, decisiveness is not. When the outer sign (Libra) and inner sign (Libra) differ in element, people who only know you casually often misread you; those close to you meet the Navamsa.
     [2] Moon, Mars, Saturn are 'Vargottama' — in the same sign in both the birth chart and the Navamsa. A Vargottama planet keeps its promises: what it shows on the surface is what it actually is underneath, and it holds steady under pressure.
  == Aspects on your rising sign — who edits the personality
     [1] Planets influence houses they do not sit in by 'aspecting' them — casting their influence across the chart to particular houses. Every planet aspects the 7th house from itself, and Mars, Jupiter, Saturn, Rahu and Ketu each have extra special aspects. The following planets aspect your rising sign, and each one edits the personality others meet:
     [2] Jupiter aspects your rising sign from your 9th house — Jupiter's special 5th-house aspect — its blessing on intelligence, good counsel and children.
     [3] Rahu aspects your rising sign from your 9th house — Rahu's special 5th-house aspect — an amplifier of intelligence and of desire.
     [4] Each of these adds its own colour on top of the rising sign itself. If people describe you in ways that do not quite match the rising-sign portrait above, one of these planets is usually why.
  == How you are seen vs how you are (Arudha Lagna)
     [1] The Arudha Lagna is the point in the chart that stands for your public image — how the world perceives you, as distinct from how you actually are. Yours falls in Sagittarius, while your actual rising sign is Libra. So the public image runs on Sagittarius's wavelength — expansive, principled and restless for meaning, needing both a horizon and a doctrine — while the person underneath operates as Libra. The gap is not dishonesty; it is simply that your reputation forms around the themes of your 3rd house. Knowing which conversations are about the image and which are about you is a lifelong advantage.
  == Yoga signatures in the personality
     [1] A 'yoga' is a named combination of planets that the classical texts single out as producing a specific result. One combination in your chart stamps itself directly on the character: Chandra-Mangala Yoga.
     [2] Chandra-Mangala Yoga — Moon and Mars conjoin — an earning combination: entrepreneurial drive fused with instinct. Wealth through self-effort, with a temper that needs channels, not suppression.
  == Which planets work for you, and which against
     [1] Which planets help you and which hinder you is not fixed — it depends on your rising sign, because the rising sign decides which houses each planet rules. A planet that rules good houses for Libra rising works in your favour whatever its natural reputation; one that rules difficult houses works against you even if it is a 'good' planet in general.
     [2] Saturn is the yogakaraka for Libra rising — the single most helpful planet your rising sign can have, because it rules one of the corner houses and one of the fortunate houses at the same time. Saturn — in your 8th house, in a sign neutral to it, strength 35 out of 100 (weak). Its condition is the best single predictor of how far your personality converts into standing in the world.
     [3] Working for you: Saturn — in your 8th house, in a sign neutral to it, strength 35 out of 100 (weak); Mercury — in your 3rd house, in an enemy's sign, strength 27 out of 100 (afflicted); Venus — in your 2nd house, in a friend's sign, strength 28 out of 100 (afflicted). The traits these planets govern are the ones you can lean on without second-guessing, and their main periods (dashas) feel like the personality working as designed.
     [4] Working against you: Jupiter — in your 9th house, in a great enemy's sign, strength 22 out of 100 (afflicted); Sun — in your 3rd house, in a sign neutral to it, strength 43 out of 100 (weak); Mars — in your 5th house, in a friend's sign, strength 52 out of 100 (moderate). These are not evil planets — they are the ones whose house rulerships pull against your rising sign's interests. Where one of them is strong, you have a talent that repeatedly gets you into trouble; where weak, a blind spot you rarely notice.
     [5] Neutral: Moon — in your 5th house, in a friend's sign, strength 49 out of 100 (moderate). These take their colour from whatever they sit with rather than carrying an agenda of their own.

--- houses ---
  == 1st House — Libra  occupied=false lord=Ve planets=[] aspects=[Ju:5,Ra:5]
     [1] Your 1st house falls in Libra. This is the part of the chart that covers self, body, vitality, temperament and the overall direction of life. No planet sits in it. That is not a weakness in itself — most houses in most charts are empty. An empty house is read the way the classical texts prescribe: by the condition of the planet that rules it, and by the planets that aspect it from elsewhere.
     [2] Libra is ruled by Venus, so Venus is the ruler of your 1st house — the planet whose condition decides how this area of life goes. Venus sits in your 2nd house at 29°32' Scorpio, which is a friend's sign, where it is comfortable. It is also combust (so close to the Sun that its light is drowned out). Counting from the 1st house itself, Venus stands 2 places along — the 2nd from its own house, a workable but unremarkable position that neither helps nor hurts. Because the ruler sits in the 2nd, the affairs of your 1st house get worked out mostly through wealth — that is where they actually play out in daily life. Its overall strength — a combined score from its sign, house, aspects and other factors — comes to 28 out of 100 (afflicted), which is low. The 1st house is under-resourced at its ruler, and its affairs need conscious support from you before they hold weight.
     [3] The ruler, Venus, also falls in the star-group (nakshatra) called Jyeshtha, in its 4th quarter. Each nakshatra has its own ruling planet and its own flavour — here the ruler is Mercury, and the flavour is: eldest-born intensity; protective authority won through trials. Mercury, the ruler of that nakshatra, is a natural friend of Venus, so this flavour is passed on willingly and arrives in a form you recognise.
     [4] Jupiter aspects this house — that is, it reaches into it from where it sits, the 9th house in Gemini, in a great enemy's sign, retrograde. By nature Jupiter is a gentle planet (a 'benefic'), and for your rising sign it is a planet whose house rulerships pull against your interests, whatever its natural character (a 'functional malefic'). The aspect landing here is Jupiter's special 5th-house aspect — its blessing on intelligence, good counsel and children. Jupiter's drishti on the Lagna is among the finest protections in a chart: an optimistic, ethical temperament, a well-made body, and a habit of landing on one's feet. Weight, literal and metaphorical, is the only excess. The good intent is there, but the planet itself is poorly placed, so the protection is real yet thin, and tends to arrive later than you expect. Note the split, though: gentle by nature but working against your rising sign by the houses it rules, so its blessing here comes with strings attached.
     [5] Rahu also aspects this house, from the 9th house in Gemini, in a sign neutral to it, retrograde — a hard planet by nature, and neutral by rulership. The aspect landing here is Rahu's special 5th-house aspect — an amplifier of intelligence and of desire. Rahu's drishti on the Lagna amplifies the persona and blurs its edges: unusual magnetism, an unconventional self-presentation, and an identity assembled rather than inherited. Other people's projections stick to you easily. Coming from a hard planet in an ordinary condition, the aspect keeps this house honest — pressure you learn to work with rather than damage.
     [6] The house receives 1 gentle and 1 hard aspect — a contested area whose results swing depending on which of these planets is running its main period (dasha) at the time.
     [7] In short: with no planet in it to speak for it, how your 1st house goes rests on Venus's condition in the 2nd and on the aspects described above. Anything that strengthens Venus — its main periods, its transits, deliberate attention to what it stands for — improves this house; nothing else reaches it directly.
  == 2nd House — Scorpio  occupied=true lord=Ma planets=[Ve] aspects=[Sa:7]
     [1] Your 2nd house falls in Scorpio. This is the part of the chart that covers wealth, family, speech, food and accumulated resources. Venus sits in it, so the affairs of this house are things you live directly rather than watch from a distance.
     [2] Scorpio is ruled by Mars, so Mars is the ruler of your 2nd house — the planet whose condition decides how this area of life goes. Mars sits in your 5th house at 15°00' Aquarius, which is a friend's sign, where it is comfortable. Counting from the 2nd house itself, Mars stands 4 places along — the 4th from its own house, which is one of the supportive positions (the corner houses 1, 4, 7, 10 and the fortunate houses 5 and 9). The ruler actively backs what it rules. Because the ruler sits in the 5th, the affairs of your 2nd house get worked out mostly through intelligence — that is where they actually play out in daily life. Its overall strength — a combined score from its sign, house, aspects and other factors — comes to 52 out of 100 (moderate). Mars can carry the 2nd house, but results here follow your own effort closely rather than being handed to you.
     [3] The ruler, Mars, also falls in the star-group (nakshatra) called Shatabhisha, in its 3rd quarter. Each nakshatra has its own ruling planet and its own flavour — here the ruler is Rahu, and the flavour is: veiled healing; solitary depths, mysticism and medicine. Rahu, the ruler of that nakshatra, is neutral toward Mars, so this flavour comes through as it is — neither boosted nor blocked.
     [4] Venus sits here at 29°32' Scorpio. For your Libra rising sign, Venus rules your 1st and 8th houses — self and longevity — which makes it a planet that works in your favour by the houses it rules (a 'functional benefic'). Venus in the 2nd sweetens wealth and speech alike: money through arts, luxury goods, finance and beauty industries. The voice may literally be musical. Family life is refined; the palate is expensive and knows it. Scorpio belongs to a friend of Venus, so the placement is comfortable and cooperates with you rather than resisting. Venus is combust — so close to the Sun that its own light is drowned out. Its outward results are weakened while pride and self-image crowd into its territory; the remedy is humility, and deliberately practising the qualities this planet stands for. One refinement: houses can be counted two ways. By whole signs (used above) Venus is in the 2nd, but by the more precise method that measures from the exact house boundaries (Bhava Chalit), it falls in the 3rd. Read its concrete, day-to-day results in the 3rd house too; its strength and comfort by sign, described above, stay as they are.
     [5] Venus falls in the nakshatra Jyeshtha (4th quarter), ruled by Mercury, whose flavour is: eldest-born intensity; protective authority won through trials. Mercury, the ruler of that nakshatra, is a natural friend of Venus, so this flavour is passed on willingly and arrives in a form you recognise.
     [6] Saturn aspects this house — that is, it reaches into it from where it sits, the 8th house in Taurus, in a sign neutral to it, retrograde. By nature Saturn is a hard planet (a 'malefic'), and for your rising sign it is the single most helpful planet your rising sign can have (the 'yogakaraka'), because it rules one of the supporting houses and one of the fortunate houses at the same time. The aspect landing here is the full 7th-house aspect every planet casts — in Saturn's case direct weight, and the one that delays. Saturn's aspect on the 2nd disciplines wealth and speech alike — accumulation is slow, deliberate and permanent; words are few and load-bearing. Early family life carries constraint, later provision is secure. Coming from a hard planet in an ordinary condition, the aspect keeps this house honest — pressure you learn to work with rather than damage. And because this is the most helpful planet your rising sign has (the yogakaraka), its aspect is one of the best structural supports in your chart wherever it lands.
     [7] Every aspect reaching this house comes from a hard planet. With no gentle planet to balance them, what this house stands for needs conscious defending, and the house tends to mature through difficulty rather than ease — which also means it usually matures well.
  == 3rd House — Sagittarius  occupied=true lord=Ju planets=[Su,Me,Ke] aspects=[Ju:7,Ra:7]
     [1] Your 3rd house falls in Sagittarius. This is the part of the chart that covers courage, siblings, communication, skills and self-effort. Sun, Mercury, Ketu all sit in it, which makes this one of the main arenas of your life — a lot happens here, and it happens to you directly.
     [2] Sagittarius is ruled by Jupiter, so Jupiter is the ruler of your 3rd house — the planet whose condition decides how this area of life goes. Jupiter sits in your 9th house at 18°14' Gemini, which is the sign of a great enemy, where it struggles to be itself. It is also retrograde (appearing to move backwards in the sky at your birth). Counting from the 3rd house itself, Jupiter stands 7 places along — the 7th from its own house, which is one of the supportive positions (the corner houses 1, 4, 7, 10 and the fortunate houses 5 and 9). The ruler actively backs what it rules. Because the ruler sits in the 9th, the affairs of your 3rd house get worked out mostly through fortune — that is where they actually play out in daily life. Its overall strength — a combined score from its sign, house, aspects and other factors — comes to 22 out of 100 (afflicted), which is low. The 3rd house is under-resourced at its ruler, and its affairs need conscious support from you before they hold weight.
     [3] The ruler, Jupiter, also falls in the star-group (nakshatra) called Ardra, in its 4th quarter. Each nakshatra has its own ruling planet and its own flavour — here the ruler is Rahu, and the flavour is: stormy, penetrating intellect; breakthroughs after inner turbulence. Rahu, the ruler of that nakshatra, is neutral toward Jupiter, so this flavour comes through as it is — neither boosted nor blocked.
     [4] Sun sits here at 5°23' Sagittarius. For your Libra rising sign, Sun rules your 11th house — gains — which makes it a planet whose house rulerships pull against your interests, whatever its natural character (a 'functional malefic'). Sun in the 3rd is one of its best placements by house: courage backed by follow-through. You out-work siblings and rivals alike, writes and speaks with conviction, and takes initiatives that others only discuss. Younger siblings may live in this person's shadow. Sagittarius is neutral ground for Sun — the sign neither helps nor hinders it. What you get out of this house tracks what you put in almost exactly, with no subsidy and no surcharge.
     [5] Sun falls in the nakshatra Mula (2nd quarter), ruled by Ketu, whose flavour is: root-cutting inquiry; destroys to find foundational truth. Ketu, the ruler of that nakshatra, is neutral toward Sun, so this flavour comes through as it is — neither boosted nor blocked.
     [6] Mercury sits here at 14°27' Sagittarius. For your Libra rising sign, Mercury rules your 9th and 12th houses — fortune and expenditure — which makes it a planet that works in your favour by the houses it rules (a 'functional benefic'). Mercury in the 3rd is Mercury doubled — communication in the house of communication. Media, marketing, coding and commerce all flourish. Siblings are clever company. The mind darts; finishing what is started is the discipline to build. Sagittarius belongs to an enemy of Mercury, so it meets resistance here — a guest whose host does not much like it. Results in this house take roughly twice the usual persistence to arrive. Mercury is combust — so close to the Sun that its own light is drowned out. Its outward results are weakened while pride and self-image crowd into its territory; the remedy is humility, and deliberately practising the qualities this planet stands for.
     [7] Mercury falls in the nakshatra Purva Ashadha (1st quarter), ruled by Venus, whose flavour is: invincible early victories; proud, philosophical, water-blessed. Venus, the ruler of that nakshatra, is a natural friend of Mercury, so this flavour is passed on willingly and arrives in a form you recognise.
     [8] Ketu sits here at 3°03' Sagittarius. Ketu rules no sign of its own. It works through the planet that owns the sign it sits in, through the ruler of its star-group (nakshatra), and through whatever it sits beside — a shadow that takes on the colour of its surroundings rather than a house ruler with an agenda of its own. Ketu in the 3rd gives effortless, headless courage — bravery without the need for applause. Sibling bonds carry past-life residue. Technical intuition (code, machines, mantra) works below the level of explanation. Sagittarius belongs to an enemy of Ketu, so it meets resistance here — a guest whose host does not much like it. Results in this house take roughly twice the usual persistence to arrive. Ketu is retrograde — it appeared to be moving backwards through the sky when you were born. In practice that turns its energy inward and intensifies it: the themes of this house come back around more than once, and matters tend to conclude on the second or third pass rather than the first.
     [9] Ketu falls in the nakshatra Mula (1st quarter), ruled by Ketu, whose flavour is: root-cutting inquiry; destroys to find foundational truth. Ketu sits in a nakshatra it rules itself, so its intent reaches the world undiluted — what it promises, it delivers in its own voice.
     [10] Sun and Ketu both sit in nakshatras ruled by Ketu, which tightens their pairing: they do not merely share a sign, they answer to the same planet, and Ketu's main periods bring both to the surface at once.
     [11] Jupiter aspects this house — that is, it reaches into it from where it sits, the 9th house in Gemini, in a great enemy's sign, retrograde. By nature Jupiter is a gentle planet (a 'benefic'), and for your rising sign it is a planet whose house rulerships pull against your interests, whatever its natural character (a 'functional malefic'). The aspect landing here is the full 7th-house aspect every planet casts — in Jupiter's case a direct protection. Jupiter's glance on the 3rd raises courage into principle and communication into teaching; skills are pursued for their meaning. Siblings are a source of support rather than rivalry. The good intent is there, but the planet itself is poorly placed, so the protection is real yet thin, and tends to arrive later than you expect. Note the split, though: gentle by nature but working against your rising sign by the houses it rules, so its blessing here comes with strings attached.
     [12] Rahu also aspects this house, from the 9th house in Gemini, in a sign neutral to it, retrograde — a hard planet by nature, and neutral by rulership. The aspect landing here is the full 7th-house aspect every planet casts — in Rahu's case direct and obsessive. Rahu's glance on the 3rd gives outsized ambition and boldness in self-effort, talent for media, technology and mass communication, and courage bordering on recklessness. Sibling relations are complicated or distant. Coming from a hard planet in an ordinary condition, the aspect keeps this house honest — pressure you learn to work with rather than damage.
     [13] The house receives 1 gentle and 1 hard aspect — a contested area whose results swing depending on which of these planets is running its main period (dasha) at the time.
     [14] Sun–Mercury (Budhaditya) welds intellect to authority — administrative brilliance and articulate command. When Mercury sits deep in combustion, cleverness serves ego; with distance, it advises it. Their combined strength is just 30 out of 100 across 9.1°, so this combination is compromised — you will feel its difficulties more reliably than its gifts. Deliberately strengthening Sun is the single most useful thing to do about it.
     [15] Sun–Ketu quietly detaches the ego from its throne — authority exercised without appetite for it. Identity questions run deep, often father-linked. The strength here is incorruptibility: what you never craved cannot be used to buy you. The two planets are 2.3° apart and their combined strength is only 49 out of 100, so they share a house more than a purpose. The promise is real but muted, and tends to surface mainly during Sun's main periods.
     [16] Mercury–Ketu perceives past the syntax: intuitive analysis, mathematical or mystical shortcuts, speech that ends conversations by finishing them. Detail-tracking bores it. Best in research, jyotisha, code and any field where insight outranks paperwork. Their combined strength is just 29 out of 100 across 11.4°, so this combination is compromised — you will feel its difficulties more reliably than its gifts. Deliberately strengthening Ketu is the single most useful thing to do about it.
     [17] With 3 planets sharing one house, it works like a committee: Sun chairs it as the strongest member, the group spans 11.4° and carries a combined strength of 26 out of 100 (afflicted). The crowding is a liability rather than an asset here — the house is overloaded, and what it stands for needs deliberate, sustained repair.
  == 4th House — Capricorn  occupied=false lord=Sa planets=[] aspects=[]
     [1] Your 4th house falls in Capricorn. This is the part of the chart that covers home, mother, emotional foundations, property and inner contentment. No planet sits in it. That is not a weakness in itself — most houses in most charts are empty. An empty house is read the way the classical texts prescribe: by the condition of the planet that rules it, and by the planets that aspect it from elsewhere.
     [2] Capricorn is ruled by Saturn, so Saturn is the ruler of your 4th house — the planet whose condition decides how this area of life goes. Saturn sits in your 8th house at 16°12' Taurus, which is a sign that is neutral to it — neither helped nor hindered. It is also retrograde (appearing to move backwards in the sky at your birth). Counting from the 4th house itself, Saturn stands 5 places along — the 5th from its own house, which is one of the supportive positions (the corner houses 1, 4, 7, 10 and the fortunate houses 5 and 9). The ruler actively backs what it rules. Because the ruler sits in the 8th, the affairs of your 4th house get worked out mostly through longevity — that is where they actually play out in daily life. Its overall strength — a combined score from its sign, house, aspects and other factors — comes to 35 out of 100 (weak), which is low. The 4th house is under-resourced at its ruler, and its affairs need conscious support from you before they hold weight.
     [3] The ruler, Saturn, also falls in the star-group (nakshatra) called Rohini, in its 2nd quarter. Each nakshatra has its own ruling planet and its own flavour — here the ruler is Moon, and the flavour is: magnetic, sensual growth; fertile creativity and material fascination. Moon, the ruler of that nakshatra, is a natural enemy of Saturn, so even a well-placed Saturn has its results filtered through an unsympathetic hand — expect what it promises to arrive altered, later, or at a price.
     [4] No planet aspects the 4th house. Its affairs run on the ruler's condition alone, with nothing from outside either reinforcing or interfering — outcomes here are quieter and more in your own hands than elsewhere in the chart.
     [5] In short: with no planet in it to speak for it, how your 4th house goes rests on Saturn's condition in the 8th and on the aspects described above. Anything that strengthens Saturn — its main periods, its transits, deliberate attention to what it stands for — improves this house; nothing else reaches it directly.

     [4] Moon sits here at 15°59' Aquarius. For your Libra rising sign, Moon rules your 10th house — career — which makes it neither for you nor against you by rulership (functionally neutral). Moon in the 5th romanticises everything it touches — creativity, children, love affairs. The mind is fertile and the heart leads decisions; speculation tempts, and discipline in it must be borrowed from elsewhere in the chart. Aquarius belongs to a friend of Moon, so the placement is comfortable and cooperates with you rather than resisting.
     [5] Moon falls in the nakshatra Shatabhisha (3rd quarter), ruled by Rahu, whose flavour is: veiled healing; solitary depths, mysticism and medicine. Rahu, the ruler of that nakshatra, is neutral toward Moon, so this flavour comes through as it is — neither boosted nor blocked.
     [6] Mars sits here at 15°00' Aquarius. For your Libra rising sign, Mars rules your 2nd and 7th houses — wealth and marriage — which makes it a planet whose house rulerships pull against your interests, whatever its natural character (a 'functional malefic'). It also rules one of the two houses (the 2nd and 7th) that the tradition watches for health in its main periods, so its periods deserve a little extra care. Mars in the 5th makes passion the co-author of intellect: sharp, decisive thinking; fierce love; sports-minded children. Romance is pursued like a campaign. Speculation is dangerous — this Mars doubles the stake when it should fold. Aquarius belongs to a friend of Mars, so the placement is comfortable and cooperates with you rather than resisting.
     [7] Mars falls in the nakshatra Shatabhisha (3rd quarter), ruled by Rahu, whose flavour is: veiled healing; solitary depths, mysticism and medicine. Rahu, the ruler of that nakshatra, is neutral toward Mars, so this flavour comes through as it is — neither boosted nor blocked.
     [8] Moon and Mars both sit in nakshatras ruled by Rahu, which tightens their pairing: they do not merely share a sign, they answer to the same planet, and Rahu's main periods bring both to the surface at once.
     [9] Jupiter aspects this house — that is, it reaches into it from where it sits, the 9th house in Gemini, in a great enemy's sign, retrograde. By nature Jupiter is a gentle planet (a 'benefic'), and for your rising sign it is a planet whose house rulerships pull against your interests, whatever its natural character (a 'functional malefic'). The aspect landing here is Jupiter's special 9th-house aspect — its blessing on fortune and right living, and the most benevolent aspect there is. Jupiter's aspect on the 5th lands on the house Jupiter most naturally blesses — wisdom, sound counsel, children who are a blessing, and merit carried in from before this life (purva punya) that pays out now. Intelligence is broad rather than narrow, and you teach naturally. The good intent is there, but the planet itself is poorly placed, so the protection is real yet thin, and tends to arrive later than you expect. Note the split, though: gentle by nature but working against your rising sign by the houses it rules, so its blessing here comes with strings attached.
     [10] Saturn also aspects this house, from the 8th house in Taurus, in a sign neutral to it, retrograde — a hard planet by nature, and your rising sign's most helpful planet (the yogakaraka). The aspect landing here is Saturn's special 10th-house aspect — the aspect of duty imposed and work scrutinised. Saturn's glance on the 5th delays and disciplines the 5th's promises: children come late or few, romance is serious, and intelligence is deep rather than quick. Purva-punya here is a debt repaid before it is drawn on. Coming from a hard planet in an ordinary condition, the aspect keeps this house honest — pressure you learn to work with rather than damage. And because this is the most helpful planet your rising sign has (the yogakaraka), its aspect is one of the best structural supports in your chart wherever it lands.
     [11] Rahu also aspects this house, from the 9th house in Gemini, in a sign neutral to it, retrograde — a hard planet by nature, and neutral by rulership. The aspect landing here is Rahu's special 9th-house aspect — a distorting influence on inherited fortune and belief. Rahu's glance on the 5th makes intelligence unconventional and speculative, romance intense and rule-breaking, and matters of children a site of either delay or unusual circumstance. Speculation attracts, and must be governed. Coming from a hard planet in an ordinary condition, the aspect keeps this house honest — pressure you learn to work with rather than damage.
     [12] The house receives 1 gentle and 2 hard aspects — a contested area whose results swing depending on which of these planets is running its main period (dasha) at the time.
     [13] Moon–Mars (Chandra-Mangala) monetises instinct: entrepreneurial reflexes, protective passion, wealth built through decisive emotional intelligence. The same circuitry produces flash-flood tempers — profitable when dammed, destructive when not.      [4] Moon aspects this house — that is, it reaches into it from where it sits, the 5th house in Aquarius, in a friend's sign. By nature Moon is a gentle planet (a 'benefic'), and for your rising sign it is neither for you nor against you by rulership (functionally neutral). The aspect landing here is the full 7th-house aspect every planet casts. The Moon's aspect on the 11th builds income through networks that are personal before they are professional, and gains that ebb and flow with your circle. Desires are many and sincerely felt. Coming from a gentle planet in an ordinary condition, the aspect steadies this house without transforming it.
     [5] Mars also aspects this house, from the 5th house in Aquarius, in a friend's sign — a hard planet by nature, and one that pulls against your interests by the houses it rules. The aspect landing here is the full 7th-house aspect every planet casts — in Mars's case direct and unavoidable. Mars's aspect on the 11th makes gains the product of campaigns and networks the product of alliances. Income responds to aggression; elder siblings are strong presences — allies or rivals, sometimes both. Coming from a hard planet in an ordinary condition, the aspect keeps this house honest — pressure you learn to work with rather than damage.
     [6] Ketu also aspects this house, from the 3rd house in Sagittarius, in an enemy's sign, retrograde — a hard planet by nature, and neutral by rulership. The aspect landing here is Ketu's special 9th-house aspect — a refining influence on fortune and right living. Ketu's aspect on the 11th thins the network to a few real connections and makes gains erratic — money arrives when needed rather than when pursued. Desires themselves lose their grip, which you may mistake for failure. A hard planet in a weak position troubles the house without building much capability in return: the friction comes with little compensating discipline.
     [7] The house receives 1 gentle and 2 hard aspects — a contested area whose results swing depending on which of these planets is running its main period (dasha) at the time.
     [8] In short: with no planet in it to speak for it, how your 11th house goes rests on Sun's condition in the 3rd and on the aspects described above. Anything that strengthens Sun — its main periods, its transits, deliberate attention to what it stands for — improves this house; nothing else reaches it directly.
  == 12th House — Virgo  occupied=false lord=Me planets=[] aspects=[Ma:8]
     [1] Your 12th house falls in Virgo. This is the part of the chart that covers expenditure, losses, foreign lands, isolation, sleep and moksha. No planet sits in it. That is not a weakness in itself — most houses in most charts are empty. An empty house is read the way the classical texts prescribe: by the condition of the planet that rules it, and by the planets that aspect it from elsewhere.
     [2] Virgo is ruled by Mercury, so Mercury is the ruler of your 12th house — the planet whose condition decides how this area of life goes. Mercury sits in your 3rd house at 14°27' Sagittarius, which is an enemy's sign, where it meets resistance. It is also combust (so close to the Sun that its light is drowned out). Counting from the 12th house itself, Mercury stands 4 places along — the 4th from its own house, which is one of the supportive positions (the corner houses 1, 4, 7, 10 and the fortunate houses 5 and 9). The ruler actively backs what it rules. Because the ruler sits in the 3rd, the affairs of your 12th house get worked out mostly through courage — that is where they actually play out in daily life. Its overall strength — a combined score from its sign, house, aspects and other factors — comes to 27 out of 100 (afflicted), which is low. The 12th house is under-resourced at its ruler, and its affairs need conscious support from you before they hold weight.
     [3] The ruler, Mercury, also falls in the star-group (nakshatra) called Purva Ashadha, in its 1st quarter. Each nakshatra has its own ruling planet and its own flavour — here the ruler is Venus, and the flavour is: invincible early victories; proud, philosophical, water-blessed. Venus, the ruler of that nakshatra, is a natural friend of Mercury, so this flavour is passed on willingly and arrives in a form you recognise.
     [4] Mars aspects this house — that is, it reaches into it from where it sits, the 5th house in Aquarius, in a friend's sign. By nature Mars is a hard planet (a 'malefic'), and for your rising sign it is a planet whose house rulerships pull against your interests, whatever its natural character (a 'functional malefic'). The aspect landing here is Mars's special 8th-house aspect — the sudden, wounding one that arrives without notice. Mars's gaze on the 12th burns resources in hidden places: expenditure on disputes, secret enemies, or exertion abroad. Well directed, it is the energy of the person who works hard where no one is watching; poorly directed, it is sleep lost to unfinished fights. Coming from a hard planet in an ordinary condition, the aspect keeps this house honest — pressure you learn to work with rather than damage.
     [5] Every aspect reaching this house comes from a hard planet. With no gentle planet to balance them, what this house stands for needs conscious defending, and the house tends to mature through difficulty rather than ease — which also means it usually matures well.
     [6] In short: with no planet in it to speak for it, how your 12th house goes rests on Mercury's condition in the 3rd and on the aspects described above. Anything that strengthens Mercury — its main periods, its transits, deliberate attention to what it stands for — improves this house; nothing else reaches it directly.

--- section: marriage ---
  headline: Your marriage indications are mixed: warm significators on one side, some classical delay markers on the other. For a chart like this the windows matter far more than the average does.
  score=55 verdict=none confidence=75
  == The marriage houses and significators
     [1] Marriage is read from your 7th house and from the planets that naturally stand for partnership. Below are the two deeper views the tradition adds to that: the marriage pada, which describes the institution, and the Navamsa, which describes the daily life inside it.
     [2] Your Upapada Lagna (the marriage pada — the arudha of the 12th house) falls in Sagittarius, and Jupiter rules it, sitting in your 9th house, in a great enemy's sign. This point describes marriage as a standing institution in your life rather than as romance — the household, the in-laws, the public fact of being married. Its condition is the best single indicator of how settled and respected that arrangement tends to feel from the inside.
     [3] Your Navamsa (the D-9 divisional chart of marriage and inner strength) is where married life is actually read, and it says this: the Navamsa rising sign is Libra, ruled by Venus; its 7th house falls in Aries, with nothing in it; and Venus lands in Pisces. Treat this as the private texture of the marriage — what the two of you are like at home on an ordinary Tuesday, rather than what the relationship looks like to everyone else.
     why(0) Your 7th house is ruled by Mars, and it sits in your 5th house, in a friend's sign, with an overall strength of 52 out of 100. That is where your partnership life gets carried out — 5th-house matters and your marriage tend to move together.
     why(5) Venus is strong in your Navamsa, the chart that describes married life from the inside. Affection in your marriage tends to deepen with the years instead of wearing thin — the tenderness is still there after the novelty has gone. [BPHS, Ch.6 (Navamsa)]
     why(2) Sun is your Darakaraka (the spouse significator — least advanced by degree), and it sits in your 3rd house. The person you marry tends to carry that planet's signature — its temperament, its pace, its way of handling difficulty — closely enough that the description usually feels familiar once you meet them. [Jaimini Upadesa Sutras, chara karakas]
     why(-4) Venus is your natural significator for marriage, and it sits in your 2nd house, in a friend's sign, too close to the Sun to shine on its own (28/100). A significator under pressure usually shows up as a slower, more deliberate route into partnership — you tend to learn about love the long way, which is not the same as being denied it. [BPHS, kalatra karaka]
     why(-3) Ketu casts its aspect on your 7th house, which the classics read as a "later is better" influence on marriage. In ordinary terms: early partnerships tend to teach rather than last, and the ones you choose after about thirty hold much better. [Phaladeepika, malefic influence on the 7th]
  == Mangal Dosha
     [1] Mars stands in the 1st from your Moon, and the 4th from Venus, which technically forms Mangal Dosha — but your chart cancels it: Jupiter aspects Mars — the classical benefic override. That means the heat is there without the affliction the tradition warns about. In daily life it reads as directness rather than damage: you say the difficult thing early, which is usually kinder than the alternative.
  == What your partner tends to be like
     [1] Because Mars rules your 7th house, the person you marry tends to carry that planet's nature — you are drawn to it, and you recognise it quickly. Tradition also reads a direction from this, South of your birthplace, but hold that one loosely: direction rules are the softest technique on this page.
     [2] There are no strong foreign markers on your marriage axis, which leans the reading toward a partner from a familiar cultural circle. That is a tendency in the chart, not a boundary on your life — plenty of these charts marry across every kind of distance.
     [3] Sun, as your spouse significator, colours their temperament more than anything else here. Look for a strong Sun signature in whom you actually choose — it is usually more visible in how they behave under stress than in how they present at first.
  == What tradition offers, and what actually helps
     [1] The standard literature recommends strengthening Venus in the traditional way — Friday observances, white clothing, courtesy toward your partner. The last of those is the one that carries the weight: attention paid to the relationship is what the practice is really training.

--- section: cautions ---

  == How to read this
     [1] These are tendencies, not sentences, and none of them is a verdict on your life. Classical astrology lists them for one reason: so you can steer. That is why every caution below arrives with the thing that actually helps — a caution without a counter-measure is just anxiety with a citation.
     [2] Nothing here is medical, legal or financial advice, and none of it should stand in for a professional who can look at your actual situation.

     [3] Treat all of this the way tradition actually intends it: as a set of preferences that tilt the odds slightly and cost nothing to follow, not as rules that decide outcomes. Wearing your colour to an interview is worth about as much as the confidence it gives you, which is not nothing.

  == Part 1: Personality

       - Your Arudha Lagna (public image) is Sagittarius in the 3rd, with Sun, Mercury and Ketu. The world sees you as principled, expansive and philosophical, coloured by self & authority, thought & speech, detachment & depth.

What helps: During Mars's main period, keep routine check-ups current, insurance in force, and the basics — sleep, food, movement — non-negotiable.
       - Mercury, Venus — each sits too close to the Sun to shine on its own — what tradition calls combustion. What helps: Separate the decision from the ego.
     summary: 10 areas in your chart deserve conscious handling rather than luck. Each one below comes with something practical to do about it, and none of them is a verdict.
  == Part 7: Timing outlook
     # Now: Saturn – Mercury – Mercury
       Saturn Mahadasha May 2023 – May 2042; Mercury Antardasha May 2026 – Jan 2029; Mercury Pratyantardasha to Oct 2026; Ketu Sookshma to Jun 2026. Cross-check: Yogini Sankata–Sankata · Chara Pisces–Aquarius.
       - Main period — Saturn rules your 4th and 5th, sits in your 8th in a neutral sign, and is a functional yogakaraka for your Lagna. What is built now is built permanently.
       - Sub-period — Mercury rules your 9th and 12th, sits in your 3rd in an enemy's sign, and is a functional benefic for your Lagna. Its themes now run through fortune & belief and loss & retreat.
     # The main periods ahead
       - Saturn Mahadasha, May 2023 – May 2042 (age 21–40): Saturn rules your 4th and 5th, sits in your 8th in a neutral sign, and is a functional yogakaraka for your Lagna — a mixed period: good in some areas, effortful in others, because Saturn is a working planet without enough strength to deliver fully.
       - Mercury Mahadasha, May 2042 – May 2059 (age 40–57): Mercury rules your 9th and 12th, sits in your 3rd in an enemy's sign, and is a functional benefic for your Lagna — a mixed period: good in some areas, effortful in others, because Mercury is a working planet without enough strength to deliver fully.
       - Ketu Mahadasha, May 2059 – May 2066 (age 57–64): Ketu rules no house (a node), sits in your 3rd in an enemy's sign, and is a functional neutral for your Lagna — a mixed period: good in some areas, effortful in others, because Ketu is a functional neutral.
       - Venus Mahadasha, May 2066 – May 2086 (age 64–84): Venus rules your 1st and 8th, sits in your 2nd in a friend's sign, and is a functional benefic for your Lagna — a mixed period: good in some areas, effortful in others, because Venus is a working planet without enough strength to deliver fully.
     # The strongest and the most demanding stretches (next 15 years)
       - No sub-period in the next 15 years has both lords strong and favourable; progress comes through steady effort rather than a single peak.
       - No sub-period in the next 15 years has both lords adverse and weak.
